Result: 在D-RICO和ODinW-13数据集上验证MECAIL，其性能基本接近参数量庞大的方法，同时实现了实用、带宽高效的大规模部署，为高度特定、聚焦和临时的场景提供全面的专家覆盖。

Conclusion: MECAIL在满足严格通信约束（14.6 KB/模块）的前提下，达到了与参数密集型方法相当的性能，证明了通信感知的专家混合增量学习在边缘智能交通系统中大规模部署的可行性。

Abstract: Intelligent transportation systems require Incremental Learning (IL) to continually improve their overall performance in dynamic environments. However, most edge devices lack the computational resources to support on-device IL, requiring updates to be transmitted from centralized servers. We propose using this setup to obtain dense, specialized module coverage that adapts a fixed base model to specific spatiotemporal contexts, such as parking lots, gas stations, ferries, or construction sites. However, in order to reliably transmit these modules to the edge device, using TCP, UDP, and BTP over V2X, Wi-Fi, and 2G-5G hardware, we establish a strict limit of 14.6 KB per module to fit within the first TCP window and to minimize UDP/BTP fragmentation. We further introduce Mixture-of-Experts for Communication-Aware Incremental Learning (MECAIL), the first method that meets this strict requirement, in which each new domain or environment is served by a small expert network that adapts the base model. We validate MECAIL on D-RICO and ODinW-13, where it largely matches the performance of parameter-heavy approaches while enabling practical, bandwidth-efficient large-scale deployment. This allows comprehensive coverage by experts for highly specific, focused, and temporary situations.

</details>


### [174] [MIGA:Shared-Geometry Gaussian Representation with Implicit Amplitude Modeling for Accelerated 3D Multi-Echo MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24468)
*Jingran Xu,Yuanyuan Liu,Yanjie Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MIGA，一种面向三维多回波MRI加速重建的扫描特异性框架，通过共享各向异性高斯几何、坐标条件多输出幅度网络与显式回波相位变量联合建模，无需全采样训练数据，在多个成像任务和加速倍数下优于对比方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维多回波MRI包含丰富的解剖与定量信息，但重复的体积编码导致采集时间过长，需要对k空间欠采样。重建欠采样多回波数据时，既要利用各回波共享的解剖结构，又要保留回波相关的信号变化；同时全体积建模带来巨大的计算与内存开销。

Method: 提出MIGA扫描特异性框架，包含三部分：共享的各向异性高斯几何、坐标条件多输出幅度网络、显式回波特异性相位变量。高斯几何提供跨回波的共同空间支撑，隐式网络建模空间结构化的幅度变化，相位变量保留回波特异性复数信号信息。所有组件仅利用采集到的多线圈k空间联合优化，无需全采样训练数据。

Result: MIGA在各成像任务与加速倍数下均稳定优于对比方法，且在更强欠采样时提升更明显；在参与评估的全体积多回波方法中取得了良好的质量-成本平衡。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将共享高斯几何与隐式回波相关幅度建模相结合，对加速三维多回波MRI重建是有效的。

Abstract: Three-dimensional multi-echo MRI provides rich anatomical and quantitative information, but repeated volumetric encoding prolongs acquisition and motivates k-space undersampling. Reconstructing undersampled multi-echo data requires exploiting shared anatomy while preserving echo-dependent signal variation; full-volume modeling also introduces substantial computational and memory demands. We propose MIGA, a scan-specific framework comprising shared anisotropic Gaussian geometry, a coordinate-conditioned multi-output amplitude network, and explicit echo-specific phase variables. The Gaussian geometry provides common spatial support across echoes, the implicit network models spatially structured amplitude variations, and the phase variables retain echo-specific complex signal information. All components are jointly optimized using only the acquired multi-coil k-space, requiring no fully sampled training data. Experiments showed that MIGA consistently outperformed the comparison methods across imaging tasks and acceleration factors, with larger improvements under stronger undersampling. MIGA also achieved a favorable quality-cost balance among the evaluated full-volume multi-echo methods. These results support the effectiveness of combining shared Gaussian geometry with implicit echo-dependent amplitude modeling for accelerated 3D multi-echo MRI reconstruction.

</details>


### [175] [Spatial Action Review: A Visual Analytics Dashboard for Auditing Language-to-Action Hand-offs in Electron Microscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24470)
*Samia Mohinta,Albert Cardona*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文揭示多模态大模型在科学图像分析中"语言答案正确但空间动作失效"的静默失败问题，并提出可视化分析仪表盘 Spatial Action Review 进行审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM 被用作科学图像分析接口时，语言答案通常由监督者审阅，而点集等空间输出则直接进入分割或区域审查等下游流程。这种"语言到动作的交接"存在隐患：答案正确但配对动作漏掉下游所需标注对象时，基于答案的监管会放行不可靠的区域，形成静默失败。现有监管方式缺乏对此类失败的审计手段。

Method: 提出 Spatial Action Review 可视化分析仪表盘，用于审查电镜线粒体分析中的该失败模式。它通过"答案-动作台账"关联成对的答案-动作记录，借助任务×数据集风险图与图像区域审计视图，将聚合模式与图像证据相连，并设置可调的动作可靠性门控支持重审。审查以人机交接收尾，监督者记录动作被接受、升级、在更严格门控下保留或标记为需模型修订。

Result: 在 EM 适配版 Qwen3-VL 案例研究的 541 个图像区域中，VQA 答案正确时点动作仍有 54.4% 未通过门控，全部记录中 27.4% 为静默失败。答案正确仅带来 5.8 个百分点的可靠动作概率提升，bootstrap 区间跨零；答案正确性与对象覆盖率的点二列相关为 0.061。这种弱耦合在 753 个匹配图像区域的五种模型条件下持续存在。

Conclusion: 答案正确性与空间动作可靠性之间关联极弱，表明仅凭语言答案不足以监管下游动作。Spatial Action Review 使答案-动作失配可见，并将其与图像证据和记录决策绑定，为 MLLM 输出进入自主科学工作流前提供审计保障。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly explored as interfaces for scientific image analysis, where a visual question-answering (VQA) response may be paired with a spatial output that guides a downstream stage. A supervisor reads the language answer, while a downstream workflow such as segmentation or region review consumes the point-set output. We call this transition from inspecting the answer to relying on its point action the language-to-action hand-off. A silent failure occurs when the answer is correct while the paired action misses annotated objects needed downstream, so answer-based oversight clears a region whose action is unreliable. We introduce Spatial Action Review, a visual analytics dashboard for auditing this failure mode in electron microscopy (EM) mitochondria analysis. It links paired answer-action records through an answer-action ledger, a task-by-dataset risk map, and an image-region audit view, connecting aggregate patterns to image evidence while an adjustable action-reliability gate supports re-audit. The review ends in a human-AI hand-off, where a supervisor records whether the action is accepted, escalated, held under a stricter gate, or flagged for model revision. Across 541 image regions from an EM-adapted Qwen3-VL case-study run, point actions fail the gate in 54.4% of records with a correct VQA response, and 27.4% of all records are silent failures. A correct answer is associated with only a 5.8-percentage-point higher probability of a reliable action, with a bootstrap interval spanning zero; the point-biserial correlation between answer correctness and object coverage is 0.061. This weak coupling persists across five model conditions on 753 matched image regions. Spatial Action Review makes answer-action mismatches visible and ties them to image evidence and a recorded decision before MLLM outputs enter autonomous scientific workflows.

</details>


### [176] [STA-TFM: Spatio-Temporal Aggregation Across Views TransForMer for Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24482)
*Mena Kamel,Natalie Won,Amrut Sarangi,Sven Jager,Albert Pla Planas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: STA-TFM是一种融合时空信息的Transformer多视角3D人体姿态估计架构，通过DSTformer提取单视角特征并用融合Transformer聚合跨视角信息，同时提出可控的多视角数据生成流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目3D人体姿态估计受困于深度歧义、遮挡和时序一致性；多视角方法虽精度更高但通常需要复杂设置。此外，多视角训练数据稀缺。

Method: 提出STA-TFM，基于Transformer融合空间与时间信息：用DSTformer作为单目特征提取器捕获视角内长程姿态依赖，再用融合Transformer跨视角聚合信息生成一致的3D估计；并提出可将任意3D姿态数据集转化为多视角设置的数据生成流程，参数可控。

Result: 在多个数据集上优于现有无需相机参数的多视角方法：DHP19上MPJPE和MPJVE分别降低50.9%和49.5%；HAA4D上分别降低6.7%和7.7%；TotalCapture上MPJPE降低15.2%。可处理带噪和缺失的2D输入。

Conclusion: STA-TFM在无需相机参数的多视角3D姿态估计中取得领先性能，具备鲁棒性，有望应用于健康监测、运动评估和沉浸式技术。代码、检查点和数据已公开。

Abstract: Monocular 3D human pose estimation (HPE) remains challenging due to depth ambiguity, occlu- sions, and the need for temporal consistency. While multi-view methods provide superior accuracy over monocular approaches, they often require complex setups. We introduce STA-TFM, a transformer-based architecture that combines spatial and temporal information for multi-view pose estimation. The approach leverages DSTformer, a monocular feature extractor, to capture long-range pose dependencies within each view. A fusion transformer then aggregates information across views to produce coherent 3D estimates. To address training data scarcity, we use a data generation pipeline that transforms any existing 3D pose dataset into multi-view setups with controllable parameters. Experiments on various datasets demonstrate that STA-TFM outperforms existing camera-parameter-free multi-view methods. STA-TFM achieves 50.9% and 49.5% reductions in mean per joint position error (MPJPE) and mean per joint velocity error (MPJVE) on the DHP19 dataset. Furthermore, it achieves 6.7% and 7.7% respective reductions on HAA4D, and a 15.2% MPJPE reduction on TotalCapture. STA-TFM handles noisy and missing 2D inputs, supporting potential deployment in healthcare monitoring, athletic assessment, and immersive technologies. Code, training checkpoints, and data are available at https://zenodo.org/records/22832620.

</details>


### [177] [VPRune: Efficient Training-free Pre-LLM Visual Token Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24485)
*Guangchuan Lv,Dianxing Shi,Dingjie FU*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练的 VPRune 视觉 token 剪枝框架，通过视觉多样性选择、相似度回收与位置保持恢复，在激进压缩下实现更优的精度-压缩权衡，并在边缘设备上降低推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型（LVLM）推理成本高，视觉 token 剪枝可降本，但激进剪枝会显著掉性能。作者指出三个退化原因：文本引导的选择偏差、被丢弃 token 的信息损失、序列压缩带来的位置失真。

Method: VPRune 是一个免训练的 pre-LLM 剪枝框架，包含三部分：仅基于视觉的多样性选择、相似度引导的 token 回收、以及保持位置的恢复机制。

Result: 在 FastVLM-1.5B 上于多个视觉语言基准测试中取得良好的精度-压缩权衡，激进压缩下优势尤为明显；边缘设备评测显示其能有效降低端到端推理延迟，同时保持较优任务性能。

Conclusion: VPRune 通过针对剪枝退化原因的设计，在免训练前提下实现高效压缩，适用于资源受限的 LVLM 部署场景。

Abstract: Visual token pruning is a promising approach to reducing the inference cost of large vision-language models (LVLMs), yet aggressive token reduction often causes substantial performance degradation. We identify three key factors behind this degradation: text-guided selection bias, information loss from discarded tokens, and positional distortion caused by sequence compaction. Based on these observations, we propose \textbf{VPRune}, a training-free pre-LLM pruning framework consisting of visual-only diversity selection, similarity-guided token recycling, and position-preserving restoration. Experiments on FastVLM-1.5B across multiple vision-language benchmarks demonstrate that VPRune achieves a favorable accuracy--compression trade-off, with particularly pronounced advantages under aggressive compression. Furthermore, evaluations on edge-device show that VPRune effectively reduces end-to-end inference latency while maintaining superior task performance, demonstrating its practicality for resource-constrained LVLM deployment.

</details>


### [178] [AgentSTAR: Agentic Shape Tracking and Reconstruction from Monocular Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24487)
*Kirill Mazur,Nikita Karaev,Matthew Chang,Jitendra Malik,Nur Muhammad "Mahi'' Shafiullah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于智能体分析-合成（agentic analysis-by-synthesis）的视频形状重建与跟踪方法，通过推断结构化3D物体模型（几何+运动学结构）并以其优化跟踪，在ARCTIC和HOT3D数据集上超越多个最先进基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常先估计稠密像素对应关系，再从中恢复物体运动，这种基于像素匹配的范式在面临大幅运动、关节运动及严重遮挡时表现受限。作者希望通过直接推断结构化3D物体模型来规避对像素匹配目标的依赖。

Method: 方法采用智能体分析-合成框架：由一个视觉-语言模型（VLM）智能体在渲染-比较（render-and-compare）循环中迭代优化形状或广义姿态，将粗略的视觉推理与数值姿态优化相结合，以实现精确的状态估计，并基于该结构化模型随时间优化物体跟踪估计。

Result: 在ARCTIC数据集上，该方法在关节物体跟踪方面显著优于最先进的3D点跟踪基线；在HOT3D数据集上，其性能优于所有被评估的刚体物体跟踪基线。

Conclusion: 通过结构化3D建模与VLM智能体驱动的分析-合成优化，该方法能够在大幅运动、关节运动和严重遮挡场景下实现鲁棒跟踪，且不依赖像素匹配目标，验证了结构化建模范式在形状重建与跟踪任务上的有效性。

Abstract: In this work, we present a method for shape reconstruction and tracking from video via agentic analysis-by-synthesis. Unlike prior methods which first estimate dense pixel correspondences and then recover object motion from them, our method infers a structured 3D object model, including its geometry and kinematic structure, and uses this model to optimise object track estimates over time. In our optimisation loop, a Vision-Language Model (VLM) agent iteratively refines shape or generalised pose through a render-and-compare loop, combining coarse visual reasoning with numerical pose optimisation for precise state estimation. This structured formulation enables our method to track through large motion, articulation, and severe occlusion without relying on pixel-matching objectives. Quantitatively, on ARCTIC, our method substantially outperforms state-of-the-art 3D point-tracking baselines for articulated objects, and on HOT3D it outperforms all evaluated rigid-object tracking baselines.

</details>


### [179] [Identity-Consistent Analysis of Long-Shot Windsurfing Video: A Domain-Specific Offline Tracking System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24492)
*Bertil Braun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出长镜头风帆冲浪视频离线分析系统，检测选手并保守形成局部轨迹片段，结合相机补偿运动与前景掩码帆色描述子进行全局关联，用两个姿态关键点驱动选手相对虚拟相机输出稳定视频。在21段视频、41,004个观测上达0.957精度、0.918召回、0.937 F1，优于OC-SORT（0.792）与BoT-SORT（0.828）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长镜头风帆冲浪视频存在小目标、大幅摇镜、长时间重叠和背景快速变化等难点；所需输出不是通用多目标跟踪轨迹，而是每位冲浪者各自独立且稳定的选手相对视频，一次身份错误合并就会使结果失效，因此需要高精度、低碎片化的跟踪方案。

Method: 构建离线分析系统：检测冲浪者，形成保守的局部轨迹片段；利用相机补偿运动和前景掩码的帆色描述子进行全局轨迹关联；使用刚性结构上的两个姿态关键点驱动选手相对虚拟相机。

Result: 在21段人工重建的开发视频（41,004个保留观测）的固定观测协议上，系统达到0.957成对精度、0.918召回、0.937 F1；OC-SORT为0.792 F1，BoT-SORT为0.828 F1。相比OC-SORT，碎片化冗余从845降至42，但95条输出轨迹中有9条混杂了选手身份，影响21段视频中的7段。

Conclusion: 该系统在长镜头风帆冲浪场景下显著优于现有跟踪方法，大幅降低碎片化，仍存在少量身份混叠错误，是当前主要失效来源。

Abstract: Long-shot windsurfing video combines small targets, large camera pans, prolonged overlaps, and rapidly changing backgrounds. The desired output is not a generic MOT trace but a separate, stable rider-relative video for each surfer; one false identity merge can invalidate an otherwise useful result. We present an offline analysis system that detects surfers, forms conservative local tracklets, links them globally with camera-compensated motion and a foreground-masked sail-color descriptor, and uses two pose keypoints on the rig to drive a rider-relative virtual camera. The tracking stage is evaluated on 21 manually reconstructed development videos containing 41,004 retained observations. On this fixed-observation protocol, the production system achieves 0.957 pairwise precision, 0.918 recall, and 0.937 F1, compared with 0.792 F1 for OC-SORT and 0.828 for BoT-SORT. Compared with OC-SORT, it reduces fragmentation excess from 845 to 42, but nine of its 95 output tracks mix rider identities and these errors affect seven of the 21 videos.

</details>


### [180] [CMAMBADEPTH: Self-supervised Monocular Depth Estimation with Channel Mamba and Hybrid Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24494)
*Xuezhi Xiang,Jiayao Liu,Heqi Xiang,Yuqi Hu,Yiming Chen,Shanjun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CMambaDepth自监督单目深度估计框架，通过通道选择性状态传播实现高效多尺度特征融合与细粒度上下文建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督单目深度估计方法存在跨尺度信息交互效率低、难以平衡局部与全局空间建模的瓶颈。

Method: 采用双向通道Mamba（Bi-CMamba）对齐编码器跨尺度特征并实现有序尺度组间双向信息交换；单向通道Mamba（Uni-CMamba）逐步聚合解码器特征并通过组选择机制保留细粒度尺度组；引入混合注意力模块（HAM）结合大核局部上下文与曼哈顿自注意力实现互补空间建模。

Result: 在KITTI上AbsRel为0.094、RMSE为4.156，DDAD上AbsRel为0.140；NYUv2零样本跨数据集泛化测试AbsRel为0.232，较基线RA-Depth提升7.2%。

Conclusion: CMambaDepth在自监督单目深度估计任务上取得极具竞争力的性能，并展现良好的跨数据集泛化能力。

Abstract: Accurate monocular depth estimation serves as a core enabler for single camera scene understanding. However, existing self-supervised monocular depth estimation methods generally suffer from the bottleneck of inefficient cross-scale information interaction and difficulty in balancing local and global spatial modeling. In this paper, we propose CMambaDepth, a self-supervised framework that achieves efficient multi-scale feature fusion and fine-grained contextual modeling via channel-wise selective state propagation. Specifically, Bidirectional Channel Mamba (Bi-CMamba) aligns encoder features across scales and enables bidirectional information exchange among ordered scale groups. Unidirectional Channel Mamba (Uni-CMamba) progressively aggregates decoder features and retains fine-grained scale groups through a group selection mechanism for subsequent fusion. Furthermore, a Hybrid Attention Module (HAM) is introduced to combine large-kernel local context and Manhattan self-attention for complementary spatial modeling. Experimental results demonstrate that our method achieves highly competitive performance. Specifically, our model achieves an AbsRel of 0.094 and an RMSE of 4.156 on KITTI, and an AbsRel of 0.140 on DDAD. In the zero-shot cross-dataset generalization test on NYUv2, it attains an AbsRel of 0.232, outperforming the baseline RA-Depth by 7.2%.

</details>


### [181] [0.5\%>100\%: Bidirectional Reciprocal Learning for Referring Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24510)
*Xiaoqiang Lu,Licheng Jiao,Lingling Li,Yuting Yang,Long Sun,Wenping Ma,Xu Liu,Fang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于适配器的参数高效微调框架BRL，通过双向互惠学习实现跨模态交互，在RIS任务上以不到0.5%骨干参数更新取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将视觉基础模型适配到指代图像分割（RIS）通常需全量微调以实现精确的视觉-语言对齐，计算开销大且有灾难性遗忘风险。现有PEFT方法虽降低训练成本，但多在各模态内独立运作或仅关注语言到视觉的单向引导，忽略了渐进式跨模态交互与视觉反馈对文本的细化作用。

Method: 提出双向互惠学习（BRL）适配器框架，在冻结基础模型的token-mixing和channel-mixing层中实现分层双向信息流。包含两个轻量模块：互惠注意力适配器（RAA）在token级进行跨模态query-key交换，使视觉与语言token相互注意，实现细粒度空间定位；互惠门控适配器（RGA）在通道级生成跨模态门控信号，用一模态的全局语义上下文自适应重校准另一模态的通道激活。

Result: 在RefCOCO、RefCOCO+、RefCOCOg基准上，BRL超越既有RIS方法，达到SOTA性能，且骨干参数更新量少于0.5%。

Conclusion: BRL通过双向、分层的跨模态交互有效提升RIS性能，同时保持极高的参数效率，验证了双向互惠学习在PEFT框架中的有效性与优越性。

Abstract: Recent advances in vision foundation models (VFMs) have shown remarkable capabilities across diverse unimodal visual tasks. However, adapting VFMs to referring image segmentation (RIS) typically necessitates precise vision-language alignment via full fine-tuning, incurring substantial computational overhead and risking catastrophic forgetting. While existing parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods enable safe knowledge transfer with minimal training costs, they predominantly operate independently within individual modalities or focus exclusively on unidirectional guidance from language to vision, overlooking progressive cross-modal interaction and visual feedback for textual refinement. To address these limitations, we propose Bidirectional Reciprocal Learning (BRL), a novel adapter-based PEFT framework that facilitates hierarchical, bidirectional information flow within both token-mixing and channel-mixing layers of frozen foundation models. Specifically, BRL introduces two complementary lightweight modules. The Reciprocal Attention Adapter (RAA) performs cross-modal query-key exchanges at the token level, enabling visual and linguistic tokens to mutually attend to each other for fine-grained spatial grounding. The Reciprocal Gate Adapter (RGA) generates cross-modal gating signals at the channel level, allowing global semantic context from one modality to adaptively recalibrate channel activations of the other. Extensive experiments on RefCOCO, RefCOCO+, and RefCOCOg benchmarks demonstrate the superiority of BRL over prior RIS methods, achieving state-of-the-art performance while requiring less than 0.5% backbone parameter updates. Code and models will be released at https://github.com/xiaoqiang-lu/BRL.

</details>


### [182] [Preoperative Prediction of Microvascular Invasion in Hepatocellular Carcinoma by Integrating Multimodal Ultrasound and Clinical Data: A Multicenter Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24524)
*Jun Cheng,Yuanyuan Kong,Qing Huang,Xiaotong Tan,Licong Dong,Yulong Han,Wufeng Xue,Ruobing Huang,Dong Ni,Qi Yang,Jie Yu,Ping Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究开发并验证了一种整合多模态超声（BUS、CDFI、DCE-US）与临床信息的深度学习模型，用于肝细胞癌术前微血管侵犯（MVI）预测，外部验证AUC达0.8953。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MVI可预测肝细胞癌的复发与生存，但传统上依赖术后组织病理学诊断。研究旨在构建术前无创预测模型，以支持术前风险分层和个体化临床决策。

Method: 多中心研究纳入8个中心共489例HCC患者，均具备B超、彩色多普勒、动态对比增强超声及临床信息。7个中心（n=421）用于建模并采用五折交叉验证，剩余1个中心（n=68）作为独立外部验证。模型采用模态特异性编码器、血流动力学时序变化模块（捕捉DCE-US双向灌注变化）以及表征一致性学习模块，在Transformer融合前对齐异构超声表征。

Result: 外部验证中，DCE-US单模态AUC最高（0.8545±0.0198），优于临床信息（0.6715±0.0156）、CDFI（0.6435±0.0344）和BUS（0.6087±0.0417）。像素差分采样与所提时序模块优于其他采样及视频表征方法。全模型表现最佳，AUC为0.8953±0.0180，准确率81.18%±2.83%，敏感性86.40%±6.69%，特异性78.14%±6.28%。

Conclusion: 整合多模态超声与临床信息可实现有前景的HCC术前MVI预测。DCE-US是主要预测信息来源，BUS、CDFI和临床信息提供互补价值，该框架有望支持术前风险分层与个体化临床决策。

Abstract: Background: Microvascular invasion (MVI) predicts recurrence and survival in hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) but requires postoperative histopathology for diagnosis. We developed and validated a model integrating multimodal ultrasound and clinical data for preoperative MVI prediction. Methods: This multicenter study included 489 patients with HCC from eight centers. All patients had B-mode ultrasound (BUS), color Doppler flow imaging (CDFI), dynamic contrast-enhanced ultrasound (DCE-US), and clinical information. Data from seven centers (n = 421) were used for model development with five-fold cross-validation; data from the remaining center (n = 68) formed an independent external validation cohort. The proposed multimodal information fusion network used modality-specific encoders, a hemodynamic temporal change module for bidirectional DCE-US perfusion changes, and a representation consistency learning module to align heterogeneous ultrasound representations before Transformer-based fusion. Results: In external validation, DCE-US achieved the highest single-modality area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC; 0.8545+/-0.0198), versus clinical information (0.6715+/-0.0156), CDFI (0.6435+/-0.0344), and BUS (0.6087+/-0.0417). Pixel-difference sampling and the proposed temporal module outperformed alternative sampling and video representation methods. The full model achieved the best performance, with an AUC of 0.8953+/-0.0180, accuracy of 81.18%+/-2.83%, sensitivity of 86.40%+/-6.69%, and specificity of 78.14%+/-6.28. Conclusions: Integrating multimodal ultrasound and clinical information enabled promising preoperative MVI prediction in HCC. DCE-US was the main source of predictive information, while BUS, CDFI, and clinical information provided complementary value. The proposed framework may support preoperative risk stratification and individualized clinical decision-making.

</details>


### [183] [ME-VLM:A Unified VLM for Embodied Cognition and Agent Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24526)
*Foundation Model,Li Auto Inc*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 ME-VLM 统一视觉语言模型（4B 与 35B-A3B 两版本），融合具身认知与多模态智能体能力，通过能力注入、分专家强化学习与多教师同策略蒸馏训练，在具身、智能体、自动驾驶与导航任务上表现有竞争力，并实现端侧高效部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理人工智能要求模型将视觉与语言理解落地到真实环境，同时需要考虑环境约束与执行反馈。现有模型在物理感知、时空推理以及数字与物理环境中的规划、交互、结果评估方面仍存在不足，因此需要一个统一模型整合具身认知与多模态智能体能力。

Method: 构建覆盖具身任务与多模态智能体任务的训练数据，包含执行观测与反馈以支持结果评估和决策优化。训练流程包括三步：具身能力注入；对具身专家与多模态智能体专家分别进行强化学习；通过多教师同策略蒸馏将二者互补能力整合进单一模型。模型提供 4B 与 35B-A3B 两个版本。面向端侧部署采用视觉 token 压缩、W4A8 量化与软硬件协同优化。

Result: 在具身基准与智能体基准上均取得有竞争力的性能，并在自动驾驶与具身导航任务上表现良好。端侧部署方面，4B 版本可在 M100 上实现设备端推理，预填充延迟由 400 ms 降至 188 ms。

Conclusion: ME-VLM 通过统一的训练范式将具身认知与多模态智能体能力融合于单一模型，兼顾多类基准与真实任务表现，并借助压缩、量化与软硬件协同优化实现可用的端侧推理，为物理人工智能提供了可行的模型与部署方案。

Abstract: Physical AI requires models to ground visual and linguistic understanding in real-world environments while accounting for environmental constraints and execution feedback. We introduce MachEmbodied-VLM (ME-VLM), a unified vision-language model with two variants, 4B and 35B-A3B, that brings together embodied cognition and multimodal agent capabilities. Our work emphasizes physical perception and spatiotemporal reasoning, together with planning, interaction, and outcome assessment in both digital and physical environments. We construct training data spanning embodied and multimodal agent tasks, including execution observations and feedback to support outcome assessment and decision refinement. The training pipeline comprises embodied capability injection, separate reinforcement learning of embodied and multimodal-agent experts, and multi-teacher on-policy distillation that consolidates their complementary capabilities into a single model. Experiments show competitive performance on both embodied and agent benchmarks, as well as on autonomous-driving and embodied-navigation tasks. For edge deployment, visual token compression, W4A8 quantization, and hardware--software co-optimization enable on-device inference of the 4B variant on the M100, reducing prefill latency from 400 ms to 188 ms.
  Project Page: https://machembodied.com/ME-Brain/ME-VLM.html Code Repository: https://github.com/MachEmbodied/ME-VLM

</details>


### [184] [Dynamic Thermal Gaussians: Multimodal 4D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24531)
*Rongfeng Lu,Lifeng Lin,Xiaobao Wei,Quan Chen,Ming Lu,Yitian Xue,Yaoqi Sun,Yuhan Gao,Anke Xue,Chenggang Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出首个面向复杂场景的动态RGB-热成像重建框架，联合建模随时间变化的颜色外观、热观测与场景几何，实现高保真时空重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热成像在军事及热分析中很重要，现有3D热重建工作大多假设温度分布静态，忽略了真实环境中热传递的时间动态性，因此需要动态RGB-热重建方法。

Method: 提出多模态动态场景表示，将颜色与热模态锚定到共享几何基底以保证时空形变下的一致性；设计多模态嵌入增强各模态运动表达能力；提出多模态路由机制，保留统一的共享多模态高斯基底，并自适应生成模态专属高斯以增强细节区域表示能力；同时构建带高频温度变化的新基准数据集。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在外观与温度两方面均实现了高保真的时空重建。代码与数据集已开源。

Conclusion: 该工作首次实现复杂场景下的动态RGB-热重建，并发布相关基准数据集，推动4D热重建评测与后续研究。

Abstract: Thermography plays a vital role in military and broader thermal analysis applications. Recent progress in 3D thermal reconstruction has extended temperature analysis from 2D to 3D space, yet most existing works assume static temperature distributions, neglecting the temporal dynamics of heat transfer in real-world environments. To address this limitation, we propose the first dynamic RGB-Thermal reconstruction framework for complex scenes. Our method jointly models RGB appearance, thermal observations, and scene geometry as they change over time. Specifically, we introduce a multimodal dynamic scene representation that anchors both the color and thermal modalities to a shared geometric substrate, ensuring their consistency under spatiotemporal deformations. We further design multimodal embeddings to enhance the motion expressiveness for each modality, and propose a multimodal routing mechanism that retains a unified set of shared multimodal Gaussians as the geometric backbone while adaptively spawning modality-specific Gaussians to strengthen the representational capacity in detail-rich regions of each individual modality. In addition, we contribute a novel benchmark dataset featuring high-frequency temperature variations to facilitate the evaluation of 4D reconstruction. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves high-fidelity spatiotemporal reconstruction of both appearance and temperature. Our code and dataset are available at: https://github.com/LinLif1869/DTG.

</details>


### [185] [MIRAGE: Full-Body Bystander Privacy for Smart Glasses with Consent-Based Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24537)
*Muhammad Umair,Muhammad Danial Maqbool,Fatima Arshad Cheema,Kapal Dev,Muhammad Hamad Alizai,Muhammad Ali Siddiqi,Naveed Anwar Bhatti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 MIRAGE，一种面向智能眼镜的三层隐私保护架构，针对全身生物特征（步态、姿态、轮廓）进行脱敏，同时支持合成替换与基于同意的加密恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能眼镜的连续录像不仅暴露人脸，还暴露步态、姿态和轮廓等全身生物特征，使得即使进行常规人脸脱敏后仍可被重识别（ReID），因此需要全身级别的隐私保护方案。

Method: MIRAGE 采用三层架构，可在树莓派 5（仅 CPU 的智能眼镜算力代理）、配套手机和云端生成后端上实现；通过边界框掩码处理完整的可见身体，并保留加密恢复材料以支持基于同意的还原。

Result: MIRAGE 达到 0.948 AP 和 0.976 AR，并能准确检测完整可见身体；边界框掩码使基于轮廓的重识别降至近乎随机水平（Rank-1 为 10.86%，随机水平为 11.12%）；面对针对其脱敏姿态信号重训练的自适应攻击者，步态识别 Rank-1 从 90.25% 降至 26.20%，消除 72.5% 的识别优势。

Conclusion: MIRAGE 能在保留实用性的同时有效抑制全身生物特征重识别，并支持加密恢复，为智能眼镜提供可行的全身隐私保护方案。

Abstract: Video recording on smart glasses exposes more than faces. Continuous capture reveals full-body biometric signatures, including gait, posture, and silhouette, that enable person re-identification (ReID) even after conventional face sanitization.
  We present MIRAGE, a three-tier architecture for privacy-preserving smart glasses that enforces full-body privacy, supports synthetic full-body replacement, and retains encrypted recovery material for consent-based restoration. We implement MIRAGE on a Raspberry Pi~5 (a CPU-only proxy for smart-glasses compute), companion phones, and a cloud generative backend. Compared to prior systems, MIRAGE achieves 0.948 AP and 0.976 AR while accurately detecting the complete visible body. Its bounding box masking reduces learned silhouette-based ReID to essentially random guessing, with 10.86% Rank-1 accuracy compared with an 11.12% measured chance level. Even against an adaptive adversary retrained on MIRAGE's sanitized pose signals, Rank-1 gait identification drops from 90.25% to 26.20%, removing 72.5% of the adversary's identification advantage.

</details>


### [186] [Incentive Noise and Structural Prior Infusion for Multi-modal Object Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24539)
*Weixiang Zhou,Yuhao Wang,Xingguo Xu,Weizhen Zhou,Zhixun Su,Jinshan Pan,Cong Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文针对多模态目标重识别中文本描述存在噪声及模态间结构差异问题，提出以正激励噪声（π-noise）与结构化提示调制为核心的框架，并在三个基准上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态ReID引入文本描述以丰富语义，但多数视觉-语言方法将文本视为干净、确定的信号，忽略其固有噪声（模态不匹配短语、语义歧义表达）；同时，即使完成高层语义对齐，也缺乏显式机制来协调模态间细粒度结构差异。

Method: 提出以正激励噪声（π-noise）和结构化提示调制为中心的新框架，包含三个模块：1）语义跨模态调制器（Semantic Cross-Modal Modulator），利用以视觉和文本输入为条件分布采样的任务感知π-noise扰动全局token，实现语义引导的跨模态补偿；2）结构感知提示适配器（Structure-Aware Prompt Adapter），通过提示注入可学习的几何先验以增强空间一致性；3）上下文感知稀疏融合模块（Context-Aware Sparse Fusion），提取结构上下文引导自适应融合，同时保护身份特征免受噪声局部细节干扰。

Result: 在三个多模态ReID基准上的实验证明了该方法的有效性与鲁棒性，代码已开源。

Conclusion: 通过引入正激励噪声与结构化提示调制，可有效应对文本噪声与跨模态结构差异，提升多模态目标重识别性能。

Abstract: Multi-modal object Re-Identification (ReID) benefits from complementary information across heterogeneous imaging modalities. To further enrich semantic representation, text descriptions have recently been incorporated as an additional modality. However, recent vision-language approaches often treat text descriptions as clean, deterministic signals and overlook their inherent noise, including modality-mismatched phrases and semantically ambiguous expressions. Moreover, prevailing methods lack explicit mechanisms to reconcile fine-grained structural discrepancies between modalities, even after high-level semantic alignment. To address these challenges, we propose a novel framework centered on Positive-Incentive Noise (π-noise) and structured prompt modulation. First, the Semantic Cross-Modal Modulator harnesses task-aware π-noise, sampled from a distribution conditioned on both visual and text inputs, to perturb global tokens and enable semantics-guided cross-modal compensation. Second, the Structure-Aware Prompt Adapter injects learnable geometric priors via prompts to enhance spatial consistency. Third, the Context-Aware Sparse Fusion module distills structural context to guide adaptive fusion while shielding identity features from noisy local details. Experiments on three multi-modal ReID benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of our approach. The code is available at https://github.com/zw-absin/INSPI.

</details>


### [187] [Evaluating Transformation Models for pCLE Mosaic Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24560)
*Ahmed Aboelela,Johannes Barcsay,Jana Friedhof,Miguel Gonçalves,Alexander Hann,Katharina Breininger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文构建了带手动标注地标对应的pCLE帧对数据集，以TRE作为几何精度度量，系统评估了从平移到TPS的变换模型与六种特征匹配后端的配准性能，并指出序列级配准质量无法可靠预测最终拼接质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共聚焦激光显微内镜(CLE)可实时提供细胞级分辨率的"光学活检"，但视野狭窄，需通过图像拼接扩展以获得解剖背景。由于逐行采集、探头运动及探头-组织交互，帧间对齐通常需要非线性变换，而其精度难以量化：灵活的变换模型可拟合强度特征与噪声，导致NCC等基于外观的指标提升却未必带来真实的几何精度增益，因此需要几何上可靠的评估手段。

Method: 建立包含4名患者、14个pCLE序列、132对帧的数据集，并手动标注地标对应关系，使TRE可作为NCC的几何补充度量。评估两类因素：(1)逐步提高变换模型自由度，从平移、刚性到薄板样条(TPS)；(2)六种特征匹配后端，涵盖经典方法(Shi-Tomasi、Lucas-Kanade)与学习方法(SuperPoint、SuperGlue、LightGlue、LoFTR、RoMa)。

Result: 在组织形变下，平移与刚性模型表现不足；随机采样的TPS在所评估配置中取得基于地标的最优对齐。在学习型匹配器中，未经微调的情况下仅RoMa相对其他方法具有稳健但有限的优势。序列层面上，成对配准质量无法可靠预测最终拼接质量。

Conclusion: TRE应作为pCLE帧配准评估的核心几何指标，与NCC互补；高自由度变换模型(如TPS)在形变组织下更有效，但学习型匹配器在零样本设定下优势有限。由于成对配准指标无法推断拼接效果，拼接质量必须直接评估而非由成对度量间接推断。

Abstract: Confocal Laser Endomicroscopy (CLE) provides real-time, cellular-resolution optical biopsy but has a narrow field of view, which image mosaicing can extend to provide anatomical context. Because of line-by-line acquisition, probe motion, and probe-tissue interaction, frame alignment generally requires a non-linear transformation whose accuracy is difficult to quantify: flexible transformation models can fit intensity features and noise, so appearance-based metrics such as Normalized Cross-Correlation (NCC) can improve without a genuine gain in geometric accuracy. We therefore establish a dataset of 132 frame pairs across fourteen pCLE sequences from 4 patients with manually annotated landmark correspondences, so that Target Registration Error (TRE) can serve as a geometrically grounded complement to NCC. We assess the effect of progressively increasing the transformation model's degrees of freedom, from translation to Thin Plate Spline (TPS), and of six feature-matching backends spanning classical (Shi-Tomasi, Lucas-Kanade) and learned (SuperPoint, SuperGlue, LightGlue, LoFTR, RoMa) approaches. Translation and rigid models prove insufficient under tissue deformation, while TPS with random sampling achieves the strongest landmark-derived alignment of the evaluated configurations; among the learned matchers, used without fine-tuning, only RoMa offers a robust, if modest, advantage over other methods. At the sequence level, pairwise registration quality proved an unreliable predictor of final mosaic quality, so mosaic quality must be evaluated directly rather than inferred from pairwise metrics.

</details>


### [188] [HyperCLIP++: Fine-tuning CLIP forOpen-vocabulary Semantic Segmentation in Hyperbolic Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24564)
*Zelin Peng,Zhengqin Xu,Changsong Wen,Yu Huang,Yaoming Wang,Xiaokang Yang,Wei Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文从双曲空间的层次对齐视角解释了联合微调CLIP能提升开放词汇分割性能的现象，并提出参数高效的HyperCLIP++方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管冻结CLIP文本编码器可保持泛化能力，但近期研究表明联合微调文本与图像编码器能显著提升分割性能，尤其对开放集类别。作者希望从层次对齐角度解释该现象，即微调过程中图像嵌入的层级从图像级转向像素级。

Method: 借助双曲空间天然编码层次结构的特性，观察到微调时CLIP文本嵌入的双曲半径减小，从而更好地与像素级视觉数据对齐。基于此提出HyperCLIP++：通过缩放变换直接调整CLIP嵌入的双曲半径以实现面向分割任务的层次对齐，并引入双跨关系通信（DCRC）模块，在视觉与文本通路间同步调整，保证跨模态对齐。

Result: 在三个基准上取得最先进性能，仅微调约5%的CLIP参数。调整后CLIP文本嵌入在不同数据集上呈现相对固定的双曲半径，表明该分割任务所需的层级可能可用双曲半径量化。

Conclusion: HyperCLIP++以参数高效的方式实现层次对齐，达到SOTA分割性能，并揭示双曲半径可作为量化分割任务所需层级的指标。

Abstract: CLIP, a foundational vision-language model, has emerged as a powerful tool for open-vocabulary semantic segmentation. While freezing CLIP's text encoder is known to preserve its generalization capability, recent studies show that fine-tuning both CLIP's text and image encoders jointly significantly enhances segmentation performance, especially for classes from open sets. In this work, we explain this phenomenon from the perspective of hierarchy alignment, since during fine-tuning, the hierarchical level of image embeddings shifts from image-level to pixel-level. We achieve this by leveraging hyperbolic space, which naturally encodes hierarchical structures. Our key observation is that, during fine-tuning, the hyperbolic radius of CLIP's text embeddings decreases, facilitating better alignment with the pixel-level granularity of visual data. Building on this, we propose HyperCLIP++, a novel and parameter-efficient adaptation strategy. HyperCLIP++ directly adjusts the hyperbolic radius of CLIP's embeddings via scaling transformations to achieve a hierarchy alignment to the target task, i.e., segmentation. To ensure this hierarchy alignment is effected consistently across both modalities and preserves their cross-modal alignment during training, HyperCLIP++ integrates a Dual Cross-Relation Communication (DCRC) module that synchronizes these adjustments between the vision and text pathways. Our experiments show that HyperCLIP++ achieves state-of-the-art performance across three benchmarks while fine-tuning only approximately 5% of CLIP's total parameters. More importantly, we observe that after adjustment, CLIP's text embeddings exhibit a relatively fixed hyperbolic radius across datasets, suggesting that the hierarchical level required for this segmentation task might be quantified using the hyperbolic radius.

</details>


### [189] [Active Visual Sampling with a Connectome-Constrained Fly Model for One-Shot Hatch Recognition in Architectural Drawings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24565)
*Dmitry Kuklev*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究测试了一个经连接组约束、面向运动预训练的果蝇视觉网络，能否在不进行任务特定权重更新的情况下，被复用为建筑填充图案单样本匹配的描述子，并得出支持迁移性与主动采样价值、但不支持生物布线优势的狭窄结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑图纸通过重复的填充图案（hatch）编码材料类别，因此需要有效的纹理描述子来实现单样本匹配。作者希望检验生物视觉网络（果蝇视觉系统）的运动预训练表征是否能直接迁移到这一非运动任务上，并区分其中"生物布线拓扑"与"主动采样/运动机制"各自的贡献。

Method: 将每个 64×64 图像块沿八条扫描轨迹平移，并在 57 种细胞类型上汇总特征；查询描述子随后与每类的一条图例条带进行匹配。使用基于 CubiCasa5K 几何构建的合成基准，在 400 张开发图纸上评估。进行了等亮度朝向条件对照，与十一种像素统计量及 Gabor 滤波器组比较，并做消融：将漂移替换为重复静止帧、仅保留受体（receptors-only）描述子，以及与一个 5,888 参数的任务训练 CNN 对比。同时区分项目记录结果与重算校验，并报告一个小规模真实图纸审计。

Result: 冻结的果蝇流水线在 400 张开发图纸上达到 0.857 的面积加权准确率，使用扩展图例时为 0.910；在等亮度朝向条件下达到 0.840，而十一种像素统计量为 0.299，Gabor 滤波器组为 0.900。将漂移替换为重复静止帧使合并等条件得分下降 0.089 [0.066, 0.112]。但仅受体描述子达到 0.891，任务训练的 5,888 参数 CNN 平均达到 0.959。

Conclusion: 证据支持运动导向的生物视觉可迁移为有用的纹理表征用于建筑填充图案匹配，并支持主动采样（漂移）的有效性；但未显示生物布线拓扑本身的优势，且端到端 BIM 实用性仍是开放问题。因此作者明确将结论限定为狭窄范围，并区分记录结果与重算校验。

Abstract: Architectural drawings encode material classes through repeated hatch patterns. We test whether a connectome-constrained fly visual network, pretrained for motion, can be repurposed without task-specific weight updates as a descriptor for one-shot hatch matching. Each 64 x 64 patch is translated over eight scan trajectories and summarized across 57 cell types; query descriptors are then matched to one legend strip per class. On 400 development sheets from a synthetic benchmark built on CubiCasa5K geometry, the frozen fly pipeline reaches 0.857 area-weighted accuracy and 0.910 with an extended legend. On an equal-brightness orientation condition it reaches 0.840 versus 0.299 for eleven pixel statistics, while a Gabor bank reaches 0.900. Replacing drift with a repeated still frame lowers the combined equal-condition score by 0.089 [0.066, 0.112]. However, a receptors-only descriptor reaches 0.891 and a task-trained 5,888-parameter CNN averages 0.959, so the current evidence supports transfer and the usefulness of active sampling, but not an advantage of the biological wiring. We separate project-recorded results from recomputed checks and report a small real-drawing audit. The supported claim is therefore narrow: motion-oriented biological vision can be repurposed as a useful texture representation for architectural hatch matching, while the topology contribution and end-to-end BIM utility remain open questions.

</details>


### [190] [Applications of Neural Cellular Automata: State of the Art, Challenges and Opportunities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24595)
*Nick Lemke,Niklas Ihm,John Kalkhof,Mirko Konstantin,Henry J. Krumb,Daniel M. Lang,Ehsan Pajouheshagar,Ario Sadafi,Arjan Kuijper,Karim Lekadir,Marco Lorenzi,Carsten Marr,Julia A. Schnabel,Anirban Mukhopadhyay*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文综述了神经细胞自动机（NCA）的基础原理、架构改进、在医学影像中的应用现状，并指出研究空白与未来机遇。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NCA 是一种能在极小模型规模下实现准确鲁棒推理的新型神经网络架构，近年已发展为图像分析、合成图像生成和仿真等任务的低资源替代方案。该技术发展迅速、研究热度上升，亟需对其新兴技术进行系统综述。

Method: 综述性研究：梳理 NCA 基础原理与医学影像应用；分析原始 NCA 架构的近期改进，并评估其在效率与精度方面的表现；回顾真实场景中的实际应用。

Result: 总结了 NCA 的架构改进及其效率与精度权衡，并回顾了其在医学影像分析、分割、分类、配准、深度估计和图像合成等方面的实际应用。

Conclusion: 归纳了 NCA 的多项优势与研究空白，并分析其在受限环境下、或对鲁棒性与高效数据处理有特殊需求的医学应用中的未来机遇。

Abstract: Neural Cellular Automata (NCAs) are a new type of neural network architecture which enable accurate and robust inference at extremely small model sizes. Recently, NCAs have advanced to become interesting low-resource alternatives to convolution- and attention-based architectures for various tasks such as image analysis, synthetic image generation, and simulation. The rapid development and increased research interest necessitate a comprehensive review of the emerging technology. This review provides an overview of the fundamentals of NCAs, applications to medical imaging, as well as insights into the state of the art. We analyze recent modifications to the originally proposed NCA architecture with respect to their efficiency and accuracy. Furthermore, we review practical applications in real-world scenarios with a focus on medical image analysis, segmentation, classification, registration, depth estimation, and image synthesis. Finally, we identify several advantages of NCAs, research gaps, and conclude with an analysis of future opportunities for NCAs in medical applications in confined settings or areas that have particular demands for robustness or efficient data processing.

</details>


### [191] [Beyond Uniform Subspaces: Spectrum-Aware and Depth-Adaptive Fusion for Multi-Task Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24612)
*Ruxi Gu,Zilei Wang,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 SADA-Merging，一种谱感知与深度自适应的无数据模型合并框架，通过为不同任务分配差异化子空间容量、自适应谱保留与深度锚定，克服现有方法对任务更新统一处理的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于子空间的无数据模型合并方法对各任务更新采取统一处理，忽略了任务更新在谱特性与网络深度上的异质性。作者观察到两个关键偏差：不同任务需要不同子空间容量、对谱变换容忍度不同；同时子空间投影会引入随深度变化的失真。

Method: SADA-Merging 包含三项机制：(1) 依据谱复杂度为每个任务分配任务特定的子空间容量；(2) 依据任务可塑性自适应调整谱保留程度；(3) 施加与深度相关的锚定以补偿投影导致的失真。方法直接作用于任务更新，兼容全量微调与 LoRA 两种设定。

Result: 在不同任务规模和适配设置下的大量实验中，SADA-Merging 相比现有无数据合并方法取得了一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过同时适配任务内在几何结构与网络深度敏感性，SADA-Merging 有效提升了无数据模型合并效果，且具有良好的通用性。

Abstract: Model merging aims to consolidate multiple task-specific models without access to extra training process. However, existing subspace-based methods largely rely on a uniform treatment of task updates, overlooking their intrinsic spectral and depth-wise heterogeneity. We identify two key deviations from this assumption: different tasks require different subspace capacity and exhibit different tolerance to spectral transformation, while subspace projection introduces depth-dependent distortion. Based on these observations, we propose SADA-Merging, a spectrum-aware and depth-adaptive framework for data-free model merging. SADA-Merging allocates task-specific subspace capacity according to spectral complexity, adapts spectral preservation according to task-wise plasticity, and applies depth-dependent anchoring to compensate for projection-induced distortion. This enables the fusion process to adapt to both the intrinsic geometry of each task and its sensitivity across network depth. SADA-Merging operates directly on task updates and is applicable to both full fine-tuning and LoRA settings. Extensive experiments demonstrate consistent improvements over existing data-free merging methods across different task scales and adaptation settings.

</details>


### [192] [Video-based Surgical Skill Assessment Using Dynamics-and-Uncertainty-Aware Tree-based Gaussian Process Classifier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24619)
*Arefeh Rezaei,Mohammad Javad Ahmadi,Amir Molaei,Hamid D. Taghirad*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出融合表示流卷积神经网络与动态及不确定性感知的树状高斯过程分类器的手术技能评估流程，通过语义感知复合核和不确定性感知核提升鲁棒性与效率，在JIGSAWS和Cataract-LMM数据集上取得优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统深度学习方法在视频手术技能评估中通常需要大量训练数据、计算成本高，且对时间变化和异常运动转换的鲁棒性不足。作者希望利用潜在运动动态作为判别性表示及输入不确定性来源，在减少数据需求和计算开销的同时提升分类鲁棒性。

Method: 该流程将表示流卷积神经网络（representation-flow CNN）与基于树的高斯过程分类器结合。分类器具备动态感知与不确定性感知能力，通过引入语义感知复合核捕捉手术视频特征中的语义、光流与动态信息，并设计不确定性感知核以增强鲁棒性。潜在运动动态被同时用作判别性表示和输入不确定性来源。

Result: 在JIGSAWS和Cataract-LMM（Capsulorhexis）两个基准数据集上进行评估。在JIGSAWS的LOSO和LOUO协议（包括受试者独立的LOUO协议）下，框架取得96.9%的平均准确率；在受试者内LOSO协议下也取得具竞争力的准确率，同时显著降低计算成本。

Conclusion: 所提流程为基于视频的手术技能评估提供了一个高效且准确的框架，在减少训练数据需求和计算开销的同时保持了较强的鲁棒性与分类性能。

Abstract: The proposed pipeline integrates a representation-flow convolutional neural network with a dynamics- and uncertainty-aware tree-based Gaussian Process classifier. In this framework, latent motion dynamics are exploited both as discriminative representations and as a source of input uncertainty, enhancing robustness against temporal variations and abnormal motion transitions. Compared with conventional deep learning approaches, the proposed strategy requires less training data and offers improved computational efficiency. To further improve classification performance, we introduce novel semantic-aware compound kernels that effectively capture semantic, flow, and dynamic information embedded in surgical video features. In addition, uncertainty-aware kernels are developed to strengthen the robustness and practical applicability of the compound kernel framework. The proposed method is evaluated on two benchmark datasets, namely the JIGSAWS and the Cataract-LMM (Capsulorhexis) datasets. Experimental results demonstrate strong performance across both datasets, including the LOSO and LOUO evaluation protocols on JIGSAWS, including the subject-independent LOUO protocol on JIGSAWS, on which the framework attains a mean accuracy of \ph{96.9}\%; results under the within-subject LOSO protocol are reported for comparability with prior work, achieving competitive accuracy while substantially reducing computational cost. Overall, the proposed pipeline provides an efficient and accurate framework for video-based surgical skill assessment.

</details>


### [193] [Relationally Grounded Latent World Models for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24626)
*Fabian Schmidt,Markus Enzweiler,Abhinav Valada*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 利用交通场景图作为特权语义监督，对齐潜世界模型的视觉表征，提升自动驾驶预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜世界模型能学习预测性表征，但其状态中所保留的关系语义往往是隐含的，缺乏显式的关系结构约束。

Method: 基于LAW，从nuScenes 3D标注构建以行为主体为中心的场景图，用冻结的文本嵌入模型编码其序列化关系结构，在训练中将视觉潜表征与该语义目标对齐；推理时移除监督分支，无需场景图和3D标注，无额外测试开销。

Result: 在nuScenes上，平均轨迹L2误差从0.661降至0.622（5.9%），碰撞率从0.456降至0.217（52.4%）；且优于无结构的描述式语义目标。

Conclusion: 显式关系结构对潜世界模型的表征学习有益，场景图可有效作为特权语义监督。

Abstract: Latent world models learn predictive representations for autonomous driving, but the relational semantics these states preserve often remain implicit. We investigate whether traffic scene graphs can serve as privileged semantic supervision for latent world representations. Building on LAW, we construct actor-centric scene graphs from nuScenes 3D annotations, encode their serialized relational structure using a frozen text embedding model, and align the visual latent representations with this semantic target during training. We remove the supervision branch at inference, so it requires neither scene graphs nor 3D annotations and adds no test-time computation. On nuScenes, our method reduces average trajectory L2 error from 0.661 to 0.622 (5.9%) and collision rate from 0.456 to 0.217 (52.4%) relative to our retrained LAW baseline. It also outperforms an unstructured caption-style semantic target, supporting the benefit of explicit relational structure for latent world-model representation learning.

</details>


### [194] [FedMust: Semi-supervised Multi-task Student-Teacher Federated Learning for Multi-organ CT Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24627)
*Ashkan Moradi,Bendik Skarre Abrahamsen,Mattijs Elschot*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出半监督联邦多任务师生框架FedMust，利用各站点的有标注与无标注数据提升多器官分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多器官分割的深度学习依赖大量标注数据，但机构往往缺乏足够大且多样的标注数据；隐私限制又使得机构间无法共享患者数据。此外，标注工作繁重且专业人才稀缺，机构通常只有小部分本地数据被标注，大量无标注数据被闲置。

Method: 提出灵活的联邦半监督多任务师生框架。每轮通信开始时进行本地训练：拥有同一任务标注的客户端组成联邦，训练并聚合出教师模型；教师为各客户端所有数据生成任务特定特征。随后所有客户端组成第二个联邦，训练一个多任务学生模型（共享编码器+各任务专用解码器），以复现教师生成的所有分割任务特征。聚合后的学生模型用于更新本地教师，进入下一轮训练。

Result: 大量实验表明该方法优于本地模型和联邦单器官模型，在各客户端平均性能提升约13%。实验还验证了多任务学习与无标注数据的作用，以及该框架在放宽客户端参与所需标注数据要求方面的适用性。

Conclusion: 该框架可同时利用有标注与无标注数据，在联邦环境下提升多器官分割性能，并降低客户端参与对标注数据的依赖；代码已开源。

Abstract: Multi-organ segmentation using deep learning requires large amounts of annotated patient data; however, institutions often lack sufficiently large and diverse annotated datasets. Privacy constraints further prevent institutions from sharing patient data to overcome this limitation. Moreover, due to the labor-intensive nature of annotation and the scarcity of diverse expertise, institutions typically have labels for only a small portion of their local data, leaving the larger unlabeled portion unused. In this work, we propose a flexible semi-supervised federated multi-task student-teacher framework that leverages federated learning (FL) to improve multi-organ segmentation using both labeled and unlabeled data across participating sites. At each communication round, the proposed framework initiates local training, where clients with labels for the same task form a federation to produce an aggregated teacher model. The resulting teachers generate task-specific features for all data at each client. Subsequently, all clients form a second federation to train a multi-task student model with a shared encoder and task-specific decoders that replicate the teacher-generated features across all segmentation tasks. The aggregated student model is then used to update the local teachers and initiate the next training round. Extensive experiments demonstrated the effectiveness of the proposed method compared with local and federated single-organ models, yielding an average performance gain of 13 percent across clients. The experiments also demonstrated the impact of multi-task learning and unlabeled data and the applicability of the framework in relaxing labeled-data requirements for client participation. The code is available at https://github.com/AshknMrd/FedMust.

</details>


### [195] [High-resolution Nitrogen Dioxide Maps Reveal Exposure Limit Breaches across Europe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24634)
*Linus Scheibenreif,Konrad Schindler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究利用机器学习构建了欧洲小时级、10米分辨率的NO2浓度地图，发现现行监管监测网络低估了超标范围，约1.35亿人口所在区域被误判为合规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NO2是常见空气污染物，与呼吸和心血管疾病相关。修订后的EU空气质量指令（2024/2881）引入2030年起的日均NO2限值。然而现有监管监测网络稀疏，且粗分辨率的年均模型无法提供评估合规所需的时空分辨率。

Method: 结合地面监测站与卫星、再分析、土地利用、交通和排放数据，训练机器学习模型，生成欧洲范围内小时级、10米分辨率的NO2浓度地图，并在每个位置返回校准后的预测分布。通过与留出的监管监测站和独立公民科学活动对照进行验证。

Result: 地图解析了欧洲110个大都市区的高分辨率时空NO2梯度。重建区域内日均合规统计量后发现，91个被监管网络判定为合规的EU空气质量区实际存在超标，涉及约1.35亿人口。此外，在地图覆盖区域内，估计有9–9.4%（约2000万）人口居住在日均NO2限值超标地区。

Conclusion: 高分辨率地图为2030年限值的人口规模合规评估提供了路径，揭示了监管网络在时空分辨率上的不足。

Abstract: Nitrogen dioxide (NO2) is a common air pollutant, released into the atmosphere through the incomplete burning of fossil fuels, and associated with respiratory and cardiovascular diseases in humans. Ambient NO2 concentrations are regulated through air-quality limits assessed with a sparse network of fixed monitors. The revised EU Ambient Air Quality Directive (2024/2881) introduces a daily NO2 limit to be met from 2030. At present, neither the regulatory monitoring network nor existing coarse, annual-mean models can resolve NO2 concentrations at the spatio-temporal resolutions necessary to assess compliance. Here we map NO2 across Europe at hourly and 10m resolution with a machine-learning model that combines ground monitors with satellite, reanalysis, land-use, traffic and emission data and returns a calibrated predictive distribution at every location. Validated against held-out regulatory monitors and independent citizen-science campaigns, the maps resolve high-resolution spatiotemporal NO2 gradients for 110 metropolitan areas in Europe. We reconstruct the daily compliance statistic across those regions and find limit breaches in 91 EU air quality zones deemed compliant by the regulatory monitoring network, covering a population of approximately 135M. Beyond air quality zones and monitor locations, an estimated 9-9.4% (20M) of the population in mapped regions lives in areas where the daily NO2 limit is breached. The high-resolution maps offer a route to population-scale assessment of compliance with the 2030 limits.

</details>


### [196] [Ev-YOLO: Uncertainty-Aware Object Detection via a Unified Evidential Formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24668)
*Simon Barbarit-Gaboriau,Hind Laghmara,Rémi Boutteau,Samia Ainouz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种证据式YOLOv8检测器，将分类与边界框回归统一到同一证据深度学习框架中，实现可在Dempster-Shafer理论下解释的双重不确定性估计，并在KITTI、MUSES和nuScenes上验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在不确定环境（如自动驾驶）中部署目标检测器需要可靠的不确定性估计。证据深度学习（EDL）虽为分类提供了理论基础，但现有证据式检测器通常将证据式分类与不同理论基础的回归不确定性模型结合，造成分类与定位不确定性的理论不一致。

Method: 提出证据式YOLOv8，利用YOLOv8基于分布的边界框表示，将证据式建模不仅用于分类，也用于定位回归，使分类和回归共享同一证据框架。两者均产出可在Dempster-Shafer框架下解释的信念、不确定性和概率估计。

Result: 在KITTI、MUSES和nuScenes数据集上，该检测器检测精度与标准YOLOv8大体相当，同时提供能有效区分正确与错误检测的定位不确定性，并且在域偏移条件下该不确定性的判别能力进一步增强。

Conclusion: 通过在统一证据框架下联合建模分类与定位不确定性，该方法在保持竞争力的检测精度的同时，提供了理论上自洽且随域偏移更具判别力的定位不确定性，有助于不确定环境下的可靠部署。

Abstract: Reliable uncertainty estimation is essential for deploying object detectors in autonomous systems operating in uncertain environments. Evidential Deep Learning (EDL) provides a principled framework for uncertainty-aware classification by representing network outputs as evidence and interpreting predictions through subjective logic. However, existing evidential object detectors typically combine evidential classification with regression uncertainty models that do not share the same theoretical foundation. In this work, we propose an evidential version of YOLOv8 in which both classification and bounding-box regression are formulated within a common evidential framework. Our approach exploits YOLOv8's distribution-based bounding-box representation, allowing the evidential formulation to be applied not only to classification but also to localisation. As a result, both tasks produce belief, uncertainty, and probability estimates that can be interpreted within the Dempster--Shafer framework. Experiments on KITTI, MUSES, and nuScenes show that the resulting detector remains broadly competitive with standard YOLOv8 in terms of detection accuracy while providing a localisation uncertainty that effectively discriminates between correct and erroneous detections. Moreover, this uncertainty becomes increasingly discriminative under domain shift.

</details>


### [197] [What Makes a Good Medical Image Tokenizer? Rethinking Reconstruction and Generation in Medical Image Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24691)
*Niklas Bubeck,Yundi Zhang,Vasiliki Sideri-Lampretsa,Julian McGinnis,Jiancheng Yang,Daniel Rueckert,Jiazhen Pan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对医学图像分词器进行了系统性评测，涵盖10个模型家族、30种配置、12个数据集与3种压缩率，横跨重建、生成、潜空间几何、下游分类与记忆化五个维度，揭示了四个关键发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜扩散模型已成为医学图像生成的主流，而每个流程都依赖分词器将图像压缩为潜编码。分词器的选择决定了重建保真度、生成质量及下游分析所需表征的上限。然而，医学图像流程通常直接沿用自然图像分词器，假设其行为可以迁移。这一假设在医学领域从未被验证，而医学数据集规模小几个数量级、样本间方差也远低于自然图像，因此需要系统检验。

Method: 作者对医学图像分词器开展系统评测：在12个数据集上评估10个模型家族、共30种配置、3种压缩率，评价维度包括重建、生成、潜空间几何、下游分类和记忆化。

Result: 发现包括：(1) 图像重建与生成性能强相关，与自然图像上的既有报道不同；(2) 现代分词器几乎用满了全部码本条目，但仍留下大部分潜空间未被利用；(3) 训练集记忆化程度轻微，且更强的潜空间压缩可进一步抑制记忆化；(4) 离散量化大体上能保持下游分类性能，无查找表（lookup-free）方案是主要例外。

Conclusion: 医学图像分词器的行为与自然图像存在显著差异，直接沿用自然图像分词器的假设并不成立；分词器选择需结合医学数据特点加以考察，压缩率与量化方式对下游任务与隐私风险均有实质影响。

Abstract: Latent diffusion models now dominate medical image generation, and every such pipeline rests on a \emph{tokenizer} that compresses images into the latent codes for image generation to operate on. Thereby, the tokenizer choice bounds every downstream task from reconstruction fidelity and generation quality to the representations available for downstream analysis. Yet, medical imaging pipelines routinely utilize tokenizers from natural imaging on the hypothesis that their behavior carries over. However, this is an assumption never tested in the medical imaging regime, where datasets are orders of magnitude smaller and images exhibit far lower inter-sample variance. We present a systematic evaluation of medical image tokenizers evaluating thirty configurations across ten model families on twelve datasets at three compression factors, spanning reconstruction, generation, latent geometry, downstream classification, and memorization. We find that (1) performance on image reconstruction and generation strongly correlate, unlike prior reports on natural images; (2) modern tokenizers use nearly all of their codebook entries, but still leave most of the latent space unused; (3) training-set memorization is mild and is further suppressed by stronger latent space compression; and (4) discrete quantization can largely preserve downstream classification, with lookup-free schemes being the main exception.

</details>


### [198] [ReSTI: A Source-Grounded Audit and Repair of STI-Bench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24727)
*Pengzhan Sun,Ramanathan Rajaraman,Shiu-Hong Kao,Junbin Xiao,Angela Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文审计了时空基准STI-Bench，发现其存在系统性的坐标系、时间戳和目标描述错误，并提出ReSTI进行源数据对齐的修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时空基准只有在问题、源标注和答案选项指向同一物理量时才有效，而现有基准STI-Bench可能存在系统性错误，从而影响评测可靠性。

Method: 对照ScanNet、Waymo和Omni6DPose官方源数据审计STI-Bench，识别坐标系与时间戳错误；提出ReSTI，在明确目标、时间、坐标系、物理量和单位的前提下重建每个可恢复答案，并用源一致的几何定义替换错误标签。

Result: 发现ScanNet Grounding缺失标注与原始相机坐标系的对齐、Orientation在错误平面上测量相机旋转，以及Waymo姿态在错误时间戳上评估等缺陷；在2,064个旧问题中保留1,782个，记录282个有证据支持的排除项。

Conclusion: ReSTI为评估精确的视频时空推理提供了保守且可溯源的基础。

Abstract: Spatial--temporal benchmarks are valid only when their questions, source annotations, and answer options identify the same physical quantity. We audit STI-Bench against the official ScanNet, Waymo, and Omni6DPose sources and find systematic coordinate-system and timestamp errors, under-specified targets and times, and disagreements between keyed options and answer details. We introduce ReSTI, a source-backed revision that reconstructs every recoverable answer under an explicit target, time, coordinate system, physical quantity, and unit. Source reconstruction reveals task-level geometric failures: ScanNet Grounding omits the required alignment between annotation and raw camera coordinate systems, while Orientation measures camera rotation on the wrong plane. ReSTI replaces these labels with explicit, source-consistent geometric definitions and corrects other source-verifiable defects, including Waymo poses evaluated at the wrong timestamp. Across 2,064 legacy questions, ReSTI retains 1,782 questions and records 282 evidence-backed exclusions. ReSTI therefore provides a conservative and source-traceable basis for evaluating precise video spatial--temporal reasoning. Project page: https://github.com/pengzhansun/ReSTI.

</details>


### [199] [GraphSVR: q-Space--Aware Graph-Based Slice-to-Volume Registration for Diffusion MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24732)
*Noga Kertes,Daphna Link Sourani,Alex M. Bronstein,Moti Freiman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GraphSVR，一个考虑q空间信息的图神经网络框架，用于扩散加权成像（DWI）中的4D切片到体积配准，通过建模采集结构实现全局一致的运动估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DWI对受试者运动极为敏感，尤其在高效协议和易动人群中。尽管切片到体积配准（SVR）可缓解层间与堆栈间错位，但扩散MRI因扩散方向依赖的对比度以及需在共同参考系中对齐数十个测量值，带来了额外的复杂性，形成4D配准问题。现有方法主要依赖序列建模或成对相似性，在稀疏梯度采样或严重运动下往往性能下降。

Method: GraphSVR将切片组表示为具有采集结构的图中的节点，边编码时间邻近性、空间切片几何以及扩散编码关系。使用图神经网络预测全局一致的堆栈级刚性运动，并以自监督、零样本方式仅利用解剖参考图像进行优化，无需成对的真实运动标签。

Result: 通过全合成扩散模拟和基于真实采集的可控运动严重程度与梯度稀疏性的真实重组模拟进行评估，以网格误差（mm）和旋转误差衡量。在严重运动下，GraphSVR相比标准DWI运动校正方法FSL eddy将网格误差和旋转误差降低73%，在稀疏方向情形下增益最大。

Conclusion: 结果表明，通过图推理显式建模采集结构，可提升4D DWI运动估计的鲁棒性与全局一致性。代码已开源。

Abstract: Diffusion-weighted imaging (DWI) remains highly vulnerable to subject motion, particularly in time-efficient protocols and in motion-prone populations. While slice-to-volume registration (SVR) can mitigate inter-slice and inter-stack misalignment, diffusion MRI introduces additional complexity due to diffusion-direction-dependent contrast and the requirement to align dozens of measurements within a common reference frame, effectively yielding a 4D registration problem. Existing approaches rely primarily on sequential modeling or pairwise similarity and often degrade under sparse gradient sampling or severe motion. We introduce GraphSVR, a q-space-aware graph-based framework for 4D SVR registration in DWI. GraphSVR represents slice groups as nodes in an acquisition-structured graph, with edges encoding temporal proximity, spatial slice geometry and diffusion encoding relationships. A graph neural network predicts globally consistent stack-wise rigid motion, optimized in a self-supervised, zero-shot manner using only an anatomical reference image, without requiring paired ground-truth motion. We evaluate GraphSVR using both fully synthetic diffusion simulations and realistic recombination-based simulations from real acquisitions with controllable motion severity and gradient sparsity. Performance is quantified using grid error (mm) and rotation error relative to known ground-truth transforms. Under severe motion, GraphSVR reduces grid error and rotation error by 73% compared to FSL eddy, the standard DWI motion-correction method, with the largest gains observed in sparse-direction regimes. These results demonstrate that explicitly modeling acquisition structure through graph-based reasoning improves robustness and global consistency in 4D DWI motion estimation. Code is available at https://github.com/nogakertes/GraphSVR.git.

</details>


### [200] [MiTHras: Task-specific Hierarchical Semi-supervised Contrastive Masked Autoencoder for Mitotic Figure Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24736)
*Trinh T. L. Vuong,Simon Graham,Quoc Dang Vu,Phat T. H. Ho,Jeewoo Lim,Mostafa Jahanifar,Nasir Rajpoot,Jin T. Kwak*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MiTHras是一种任务特化的预训练框架，通过伪标签引导的图像级和token级对比学习结合掩码重建，在核分裂象分析任务上取得优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化核分裂象（MF）分析虽有助于肿瘤分级和预后评估，但现有模型对组织类型和图像采集差异较为敏感，缺乏稳健且可迁移的表示。

Method: 提出MiTHras预训练框架，融合伪标签引导的图像级与token级对比学习及掩码重建；构建TCGA-MF-Pseudo语料库，包含来自14个TCGA队列、覆盖11个器官部位的180万张以细胞为中心的图像。

Result: 在MF分类、检测、基于计数的生存预测和亚型分类上进行评估。MiTHras在全部三个MF分类基准和两个亚型基准上取得最高平均F1；在冻结编码器线性探测下比通用和病理基础编码器优势更明显；检测提升有限（受共享候选检测阶段影响），消融实验证实token级监督改善了典型与非典型分类及线性探测。

Conclusion: MiTHras能够为自动化核分裂活性评估提供稳健且可迁移的表示。

Abstract: Mitotic figure (MF) analysis supports tumor grading and prognostic assessment, but automated models remain sensitive to differences in tissue type and image acquisition. We present MiTHras, a task-specific pretraining framework that combines pseudo-label-guided image- and token-level contrastive learning with masked reconstruction. We construct TCGA-MF-Pseudo, a corpus of 1.8 million cell-centered images from 14 TCGA cohorts spanning 11 organ sites. Comprehensive evaluation on MF classification, detection, count-based survival prediction, and subtype classification demonstrates the efficacy of MiTHras. It achieves the highest mean F1 on all three MF classification benchmarks and both subtype benchmarks. MiTHras also outperforms general-purpose and pathology foundation encoders by a larger margin under frozen-encoder linear probing than under full fine-tuning. Although detection gains are modest due to a shared candidate-detection stage, ablations confirm that token-level supervision improves typical-versus-atypical classification and linear probing. These findings establish that MiTHras yields robust, transferable representations for automated mitotic activity assessment.

</details>


### [201] [Ananke: Contractive Torus Attractor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24737)
*Zhongping Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Ananke表示学习框架，将潜表示约束在结构化乘积环面先验上，并实现为Contractive Torus Attractor Networks (CTAN)，在极低参数量下于医学内窥镜与自然图像基准上取得优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉骨干网络参数量巨大、表示缺乏结构性约束，本文希望用几何化的乘积环面先验来组织潜空间，从而在保证语义不变性的同时大幅压缩模型容量。

Method: 将高维潜空间分解为二维相平面的正交直和，通过解耦双相连续流协调特征更新：斜对称哈密顿输运沿能量等值面切向移动以保持语义相位不变量，而有符号梯度耗散将横向扰动收缩至目标不变流形；对冻结参数的圆势族给出精确对数辛流（ELSF）闭式映射；并用Lie-Trotter算子分裂统一空间深度扩散与局部流形收缩，同时分析精确三角流与辛双剪切变体。

Result: 在CIFAR-100和Kvasir-v2上，CTAN以仅0.27M参数的分层模型在Kvasir-v2取得90.52%准确率，容量比ResNet-50、DenseNet-161等25M+基线低近两个数量级；放大版本在CIFAR-100上达到80.32% top-1准确率。

Conclusion: 该工作表明乘积环面先验与辛/耗散双相流设计可带来极高的参数效率，为结构化的表示学习与紧凑视觉骨干提供了理论与实验支持。

Abstract: We introduce Ananke, a representation-learning framework that scaffolds latent representations onto a structured product-torus prior, and its flagship visual backbone realization, Contractive Torus Attractor Networks (CTAN). By factorizing high-dimensional latent spaces into an orthogonal direct sum of two-dimensional phase planes ($\bigoplus_{k=1}^K \R^2$), Ananke coordinates feature updates via a decoupled dual-phase continuous flow: skew-symmetric Hamiltonian transport moves features tangentially along energy level sets to preserve semantic phase invariants, while signed gradient dissipation contracts transverse perturbations normally toward target invariant manifolds. For circular potential families with frozen parameters, logarithmic radial feedback yields the Exact Log-Symplectic Flow (ELSF), an analytical closed-form mapping with exact exponential decay of log-radius error that evaluates in a single forward pass without numerical integration. We establish local input-to-state bounds for level-set deviations and log-radius errors, and characterize the normal hyperbolicity and persistence of the ideal product torus under bounded perturbations. We further formulate the architecture through Lie--Trotter operator splitting, unifying spatial depthwise diffusion with local manifold contraction, and analyze both exact trigonometric flows and hardware-friendly symplectic dual-shear variants. Across natural image benchmarks (CIFAR-100) and clinically challenging endoscopy datasets (Kvasir-v2), CTAN demonstrates exceptional parameter efficiency: an ultra-compact hierarchical model with merely 0.27M parameters achieves 90.52\% accuracy on Kvasir-v2, outperforming 25M+ baselines (ResNet-50, DenseNet-161) by nearly two orders of magnitude in capacity, while scaled variants attain 80.32\% top-1 accuracy on CIFAR-100.

</details>


### [202] [PrismGPT: Proxy-Guided Learning for Region-Aware Photo Editing with Self-Synthesized Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24768)
*Ke Zhao,Hue Nguyen,Abhijith Punnappurath,Zhongling Wang,Iqbal Mohomed,Michael S. Brown*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PrismGPT，一个能从单张图像生成结构化、区域感知编辑计划的视觉语言模型框架，无需依赖商业黑盒工具，在MIT-Adobe FiveK和新基准SPIRE上取得SOTA，且仅使用先前最佳方法约6%的训练数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专业照片后期同时依赖全局调整和由语义掩码引导的区域局部编辑，但现有自动化方法只能部分完成该工作流。训练VLM同时诊断全局与局部美学缺陷并预测精确编辑参数，面临巨大的组合决策空间挑战。

Method: 采用代理引导学习：通过操作分解和区域感知美学排序两个较简单的代理任务，教授模型所需的基础技能；并使用基于能力的动态调度器自动重新平衡多任务训练比例，随技能掌握程度逐步将重心从代理任务转移到主编辑任务。用于监督微调的所有推理轨迹均由同一基础模型自合成，无需更强的外部教师模型。

Result: 在MIT-Adobe FiveK以及新提出的专业修图基准SPIRE上，PrismGPT取得SOTA结果，同时训练数据使用量仅为先前最佳方法的约6%。

Conclusion: 代理引导学习与能力驱动的动态调度能有效训练VLM完成全局与区域感知的照片编辑规划，自合成推理轨迹可替代外部强教师，使方法在更少数据下达到领先性能。

Abstract: Professional photo finishing relies on both global adjustments and region-specific local edits guided by semantic masks, yet current automated methods handle this workflow only partially. We present PrismGPT, a Vision-Language Model (VLM) framework that produces structured, region-aware editing plans from a single input image without relying on commercial black-box tools. Training a VLM to simultaneously diagnose aesthetic deficiencies at both global and local levels while predicting precise editing parameters is challenging due to the vast combinatorial decision space. We address this through proxy-guided learning: two simpler proxy tasks -- operation decomposition and region-aware aesthetic ranking -- teach the foundational skills the model needs, while a competence-based dynamic scheduler automatically rebalances the multi-task training ratio, progressively shifting emphasis from the proxy tasks to the primary editing task as each skill is mastered. Crucially, all reasoning traces used for supervised fine-tuning are self-synthesized by the same base model, eliminating the need for a stronger external teacher. Experiments on MIT-Adobe FiveK and SPIRE, a new professionally retouched benchmark we introduce, show that PrismGPT achieves state-of-the-art results while using only ~6% of the training data compared to the previous best method.

</details>


### [203] [Brain Metastases Segmentation for BraTS 2026 Task 1: A Multi-Architecture Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24769)
*Mahdi Islam,Musarrat Tabassum*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 比较nnU-Net、ResEncL、区域训练与Primus Transformer在脑转移瘤分割任务上的表现，并揭示后处理与标签重建中的三个陷阱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑转移瘤是最常见的颅内恶性肿瘤，约30%的实体瘤患者会发生，中位生存期仅约5.9个月。自动分割对治疗计划和体积监测至关重要，但转移灶往往体积小、数量多且大小异质，给分割带来挑战。

Method: 针对BraTS-METS 2026 Task 1，对比四种方案：普通nnU-Net基线、ResEncL（残差编码器大模型）变体、基于区域的训练以及Primus Transformer模型；采用按患者分组的交叉验证，以避免来自UCSD纵向子集的数据泄漏。

Result: Primus（基于标签）在综合DSC/NSD上表现最佳：ET 0.710/0.761，TC 0.742/0.785，WT 0.683/0.689，RC 0.531/0.436。ResEncL综合指标略低，但病灶级F1显著更高（ET：0.452 vs. 0.052）；两者概率平均集成仅部分保留ResEncL的F1优势（ET病灶级F1为0.064）。

Conclusion: Primus在体素级综合指标上最优，而ResEncL在小病灶检出（病灶级F1）上优势明显，简单集成难以兼顾两者。研究还总结了三个可推广的后处理与标签重建陷阱。代码已开源。

Abstract: Brain metastases are the most common intracranial malignancy, occurring in roughly 30% of patients with primary solid tumors and carrying a median survival near 5.9 months. Automated segmentation is critical for treatment planning and volumetric monitoring, but metastases are frequently small, numerous, and heterogeneous in size within a single patient. We compare a plain nnU-Net baseline, a Residual Encoder Large (ResEncL) variant, region-based training, and a Primus transformer model for BraTS-METS 2026 Task 1, using patient-grouped cross-validation to prevent leakage from the longitudinal UCSD subset. Primus (label-based) is our strongest individual model by aggregate DSC/NSD, achieving 0.710/0.761 (ET), 0.742/0.785 (TC), 0.683/0.689 (WT), and 0.531/0.436 (RC). ResEncL trails Primus on aggregate DSC/NSD but achieves substantially higher lesion-wise F1 (e.g. ET: 0.452 vs. 0.052); a probability-averaging ensemble of the two only partially preserves ResEncL's F1 advantage (ET lesion-wise F1: 0.064). We further report three postprocessing and label-reconstruction pitfalls we believe generalize beyond this challenge. Code is available at https://github.com/mahdiislam79/BraTS_METS_2026.

</details>


### [204] [Virtual neural networks: hundreds of souls in a body](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24782)
*Petr Hurtik,Marek Vajgl,Zahra Alijani,Vojtech Molek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出“虚拟神经网络”概念：可训练参数量保持不变，通过计算资源实现可扩展性，并将孪生网络与深度集成相结合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在固定参数规模下提升模型性能与鲁棒性，探索仅靠计算资源扩展而非增加可训练参数的方式。

Method: 将孪生神经网络与深度集成技术结合，由少量物理模型共享权重生成大量虚拟模型，集成可同时包含数百个训练模型；所有虚拟网络接收相同输入，其互联结构产生内部扰动以增强整体鲁棒性。

Result: 集成精度随虚拟网络数量增加而提升且容量不变；虚拟神经网络优于更大容量模型、典型深度集成及SWA、Masksembles等现有方法；集成中表现最佳的单个模型也超过单独训练的更大参数模型。

Conclusion: 虚拟神经网络是一种通用抽象框架，可用标准卷积网络实现，在不增加容量的情况下通过计算扩展获得更优性能与鲁棒性。

Abstract: A new concept, termed virtual neural networks, is introduced, where the count of trainable parameters is kept constant, and scalability is attained purely through computational resources. This concept is an abstract framework that can be realized using any standard convolutional neural network. It merges siamese neural networks with a deep ensemble technique by generating numerous virtual models that share weights derived from a small set of physical models. The ensemble comprises up to hundreds of trained models simultaneously. All virtual networks take the same input, and their interconnected structure induces an internal distortion that boosts the entire ensemble robustness. The accuracy of the ensemble improves as the number of virtual networks increases, without changing the capacity. Virtual neural networks outperform larger capacity models, typical deep ensembles, and contemporary approaches like SWA and Masksembles. Additionally, the highest-performing individual model from the ensemble surpasses other models trained individually, even those with a greater number of parameters. Code: gitlab.com/EnginCZ/virtual-models-public

</details>


### [205] [Toward a foundation model for forest point clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24787)
*Yuanwen Yue,Stefano Puliti,Damien Robert,Atakan Topaloğlu,Binbin Xiang,Maciej Wielgosz,Jan Dirk Wegner,Rasmus Astrup,Christian Rupprecht,Konrad Schindler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究探索面向3D森林的通用基础模型，使用LitePT为骨干，通过大规模无标注点云自监督预训练，在四类森林清查任务上系统比较了从头训练、监督预训练和自监督预训练三种策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前森林清查AI模型通常专用于单一任务、传感器和森林类型，适配成本高（标注、算力、专业知识）。作者希望验证单一预训练模型能否跨多种森林清查场景学习可迁移表征，借鉴语言模型和计算机视觉的基础模型思路推进3D林业基础模型。

Method: 以LitePT为骨干，先建立强监督基线；随后构建覆盖机载、无人机和移动激光扫描的多样化森林生态系统大规模无标注语料；采用自监督学习预训练同一骨干；在四个代表性林业任务上，于不同标注预算下系统比较从头训练、监督预训练、自监督预训练三种表征学习策略。

Result: 监督基线在森林语义与实例分割、树种分类、树龄回归基准上取得新SOTA。相比从头训练，自监督预训练加速收敛且在标注稀缺时持续提升性能；相比任务特定监督预训练，自监督预训练产生更具跨任务迁移性的表征。研究指出实例判别而非森林语义是通用3D森林基础模型的主要剩余障碍。

Conclusion: 预训练表征在标注稀缺场景下价值最大；自监督预训练在收敛速度、稀缺标注性能和跨任务迁移性上均优于其他策略。作者认为实例判别是构建通用3D森林基础模型的关键待突破点，代码与模型已开源。

Abstract: Forest inventories increasingly rely on artificial intelligence (AI) models to derive forest attributes from large-scale 3D point clouds. Current models are typically specialized to a single task, sensor, and forest type, making adaptation expensive in terms of annotations, computation, and expertise. We ask whether a single pretrained model can instead learn transferable representations across diverse forest inventory settings. Inspired by recent developments in language modelling and computer vision, we take a step toward a foundation model (FM) for 3D forestry. Using LitePT as backbone, we first establish a strong supervised baseline that sets a new state of the art on forest semantic and instance segmentation, tree species classification, and age regression benchmarks. We then curate a large-scale unlabelled corpus spanning airborne, UAV, and mobile laser scanning across diverse forest ecosystems, and pretrain the same backbone using self-supervised learning. We systematically evaluate representation learning strategies by comparing training from scratch, supervised pretraining, and self-supervised pretraining across four representative forestry tasks, under varying annotation budgets. Compared with training from scratch, self-supervised pretraining accelerates model convergence and consistently improves performance when annotations are scarce. Compared with task-specific supervised pretraining, self-supervised pretraining yields more transferable representations across downstream forestry tasks. These findings identify the practical regime in which pretrained representations are most valuable and suggest that instance discrimination, rather than forest semantics, is the main remaining obstacle to a general-purpose 3D forest foundation model. Code and models are available at: https://github.com/prs-eth/ForPT.

</details>


### [206] [Streaming Video Editing with Easy Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24788)
*Yujia Hu,Jiajun Li,Zihao He,Songhua Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SVEET：一种基于预训练双向视频扩散模型、通过自回归方式实现高质量流式视频编辑的框架，单张H100 GPU可达15 FPS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频到视频扩散方法难以支持高质量流式编辑，需在预训练双向模型上实现流式适配，并解决双向与流式模型特征空间不一致的问题。

Method: 系统回顾现有video-to-video扩散方法，提出流式适配的两大原则：主干特征解耦与条件帧独立。核心是辅助模型分支以时间独立自注意力编码源视频并注入主干对应模块；并提出解耦训练方案，强制视频可控性与模型因果性优化方向正交，实现零样本知识迁移。

Result: 大量实验表明SVEET在保持实时性能的同时实现更优编辑质量，单张H100 GPU上达到15 FPS，且无需任何辅助加速技术。

Conclusion: SVEET验证了在预训练双向视频扩散模型上仅经训练即可实现高质量自回归流式视频编辑的可行性，兼顾质量与效率。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose SVEET, a framework that requires merely training on a pretrained bidirectional video diffusion model but supports high-quality streaming video editing in an auto-regressive fashion. To tackle this problem, we first systematically revisit existing video-to-video diffusion approaches and identify two key principles for such streaming adaptation: backbone feature disentanglement and conditional frame independence. Building on these insights, we develop a novel paradigm for controllable video generation. At its core, an auxiliary model branch encodes source video inputs with temporally independent self-attention, and the intermediate features are injected into the corresponding backbone blocks for streaming-compatible control. Moreover, to bridge the discrepancy between the feature spaces of bidirectional and streaming models, we propose a decoupled training scheme that explicitly enforces the orthogonality between the optimization directions of video controllability and model causality. Such disentanglement ensures compatibility between the two objectives at inference and facilitates smooth zero-shot knowledge transfer across heterogeneous backbone architectures. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SVEET achieves superior editing quality while maintaining real-time performance, attaining 15 FPS on a single H100 GPU 17 without any auxiliary acceleration techniques. Codes are available at https://github.com/YujiaHu1109/SVEET.

</details>


### [207] [INTCORT: Training-Free Spatial Reasoning Enhancement for Vision-Language Models via Input Transformations and Confidence Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24813)
*Haoran Sun,Jingqi Xu,Yanhui Li,Enci Liu,Kaidi Xu,Yanwei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练空间推理增强框架 INTCORT，通过输入变换构造多推理视图，并用关系 token 置信度路由聚合预测，在多个基准上平均提升 10.01%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在多模态任务上表现优异，但空间推理能力仍然薄弱。现有增强方法分两类：依赖训练的方法计算开销大且存在灾难性遗忘；免训练方法则干扰模型内部机制，损害通用能力。因此需要一种既高效又不干扰内部机制的增强方案。

Method: 论文首先验证两个关键假设：合适的几何图像变换与查询反转变换能够纠正错误的空间预测；正确预测比错误预测具有更高的关系 token 置信度。基于此提出 INTCORT：通过输入变换构造多个推理视图，再依据关系 token 置信度进行路由，聚合各视图预测，全程不修改 VLM 内部机制。

Result: 在多个常用基准、多种 VLM 上的实验表明，INTCORT 显著提升空间推理准确率，所有模型与基准平均提升 10.01%；与已有工作相比，最高可提升 25.01%。

Conclusion: INTCORT 是一种免训练、不侵入模型内部的通用空间推理增强框架，兼顾性能提升与通用能力保持，且在多模型多基准上具有良好泛化性。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in multimodal tasks, yet they still exhibit poor ability in spatial reasoning. Existing training-dependent and training-free enhancement methods suffer from high computational costs with catastrophic forgetting and internal mechanism interference that compromises general capabilities, respectively. In this work, we first verify two key hypotheses: appropriate geometric image transformation and query-reversal transformation can recover incorrect spatial predictions, and correct predictions exhibit higher relation-token confidence than incorrect ones. Based on these findings, we propose INTCORT, a training-free spatial reasoning enhancement framework that constructs multiple inference views through input transformations and aggregates their predictions via relation-token confidence routing, without modifying the VLM's internal mechanisms. Experimental results on several commonly-used benchmarks demonstrate that INTCORT substantially improves spatial reasoning accuracy across diverse VLMs, achieving an average improvement of 10.01% over all models and benchmarks. Compared with prior works, INTCORT achieves superior performance with improvements of up to 25.01%.

</details>


### [208] [Mobile Imaging Solutions for Medical Diagnosis: Trends and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24814)
*Syed Muhammad Ibne Zulfiker,Tanzima Hashem,Fariha Tabassum Islam,Md Sultanul Arifin,Khandker Aftarul Islam,Nishat Anjum Bristy,Faria Huq,Priyeta Saha,Syeda Nahida Akter,Arpita Saha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文综述了基于移动设备（如智能手机、笔记本电脑）图像进行医疗诊断的现有研究，重点探讨其在低成本、可及医疗中的潜力，并对不同健康应用类别的先进方案进行比较分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 处理能力、摄像技术和移动图像分析的进步，使智能手机等移动设备日益适合医疗诊断和健康应用，尤其可为偏远和资源受限地区的人群提供低成本疾病诊断与监测。

Method: 综述性研究：调研现有移动图像医疗诊断研究，按不同健康应用类别对先进方案进行比较分析，考察其优势与局限，并归纳理想诊断工具应具备的特征、已有进展及需进一步研究的方向。

Result: 识别出各类移动图像诊断方案的优势与局限，总结出移动图像诊断工具的期望特征，并指出已取得进展的领域与尚待深入研究的领域，同时讨论应用特定及共通的挑战。

Conclusion: 本研究全面概述了基于移动图像的医疗方案及其潜力，表明其可支持低成本疾病诊断与监测，特别适用于偏远和资源受限环境中的欠服务人群。

Abstract: Advances in processing power, camera technologies, and mobile image analysis have made smartphones and other mobile devices, such as laptops, increasingly suitable for medical diagnosis and healthcare applications.
